# Biblioteca de banners REVO · Meta Ads

81 banners master (3:4) + sus versiones 1:1 y 9:16. Generado por `scripts/build_banners.py` desde `catalogo/revo-catalogo.json` (datos de revoimport.com).

Cada banner tiene: imágenes de referencia (todas de revoimport.com), prompt master 3:4 (Feed), prompt de redimensión 1:1 (Feed) y 9:16 (Stories/Reels), y textos para Meta.

---

## iPhone 18 Pro Max  ·  S/ 6,550

Link: https://revoimport.com/p/iphone-18-pro-max/

**Textos para Meta (súbelos como opciones múltiples del mismo anuncio)**

- Texto principal 1: iPhone 18 Pro Max nuevo y sellado, 256 GB, a S/ 6,550. Te enviamos un video de tu equipo antes de pagar.
- Texto principal 2: Elige Guinda, Celeste, Silver o Negro. Viene con 4 regalos y 1 año de garantía por fallas de fábrica.
- Texto principal 3: En Lima pagas al recibir. A provincia también llegamos: coordinamos pago y envío por WhatsApp.
- Títulos: iPhone 18 Pro Max a S/ 6,550 · Nuevo, sellado y con 4 regalos · Pagas al recibir en Lima
- Descripción: Consulta disponibilidad
- Botón: Enviar mensaje

### T1 · Diagrama de partes (callouts numerados)  (`iphone-18-pro-max__T1`)

Referencias: <https://revoimport.com/assets/apple/iphone-18-pro-max/guinda/guinda-1.webp>, <https://revoimport.com/assets/apple/iphone-18-pro-max/guinda/guinda.webp>, <https://revoimport.com/assets/revo/accessories/case-iphone.webp>, <https://revoimport.com/assets/revo/accessories/mica-iphone.webp>, <https://revoimport.com/assets/revo/accessories/cable-usbc-usbc.webp>

Textos exactos en la imagen: TU IPHONE 18 PRO MAX, NUEVO Y SELLADO · 1  Triple cámara. · 2  256 GB de almacenamiento. · 3  Nuevo y sellado. · 4  Case de regalo. · 5  Mica de regalo. · 6  Cable compatible de regalo. · S/ 6,550 · PIDE HOY · + Audífonos Pro 2 de regalo. Regalos sujetos a disponibilidad.

**Prompt master 3:4 (Feed)**

```text
Vertical 3:4 product feature infographic ad, clean minimalist layout on a pure white background with bold black sans-serif typography (Helvetica Neue Bold / Neue Haas Grotesk style). All on-image copy is in Spanish (Peru).

Top, centered: bold black all-caps headline in one line "TU IPHONE 18 PRO MAX, NUEVO Y SELLADO"

Upper panel: a large rounded-corner light cool-gray card. Inside, a studio product shot of the iPhone 18 Pro Max in Guinda (deep burgundy), a flat-edged smartphone with a full-width rear camera plateau holding three large lenses, a flash and a sensor, identical to reference Image 1, in 3/4 rear view showing the camera, with a second unit partly visible behind it showing the screen, positioned on the right and slightly cropped by the right edge, soft shadow beneath. Small white circular numbered markers with thin black outlines placed on the product: "1" on the camera, "2" on the screen of the second unit. On the left, matching markers with bold black text: "1  Triple cámara." and "2  256 GB de almacenamiento."

Lower panel: a second rounded light-gray card. On the left, a top-down view of the same phone lying screen-down next to its gifts: the clear transparent case (Image 3), the tempered-glass screen protector (Image 4) and the neatly coiled white USB-C cable (Image 5). Numbered markers on it: "3" on the phone, "4" on the case, "5" on the screen protector, "6" on the cable. On the right, a vertical list with markers and bold black text: "3  Nuevo y sellado.", "4  Case de regalo.", "5  Mica de regalo.", "6  Cable compatible de regalo."

Above the button, centered: large bold black price text "S/ 6,550"
Bottom: a full-width black rounded-rectangle button with bold white all-caps text "PIDE HOY"
Under the button, one line of small gray text: "+ Audífonos Pro 2 de regalo. Regalos sujetos a disponibilidad."

Soft diffused studio lighting, realistic materials and reflections, sharp e-commerce product photography, clean technical infographic aesthetic.

Reference images, in order: Image 1 — the iPhone 18 Pro Max in Guinda, front and back (product identity); Image 2 — close-up of the iPhone 18 Pro Max camera area; Image 3 — the clear transparent gift case; Image 4 — the tempered-glass gift screen protector; Image 5 — the white USB-C gift cable. Use the references only for the exact shape, proportions, color and visible details of each product and keep them identical: do not invent or remove lenses, ports, buttons, prongs, cables or accessories. Ignore and never reproduce any logo, sticker, packaging or printed text visible in the references.

Format: vertical 3:4 for Facebook and Instagram Feed. Keep the headline, every label, the price and the button inside the central 4:5 area (leave at least 4% of plain background at the very top and bottom) with at least 6% side margins, so nothing is lost when the feed crops the image to 4:5.

Brand-safety and text rules: do not show the Apple logo, the word AirPods or any other brand logo, trademark, sticker or printed label on devices, boxes, cases, cables or screens; device backs, props and accessories stay clean and unbranded. Any visible phone screen shows only an abstract dark wallpaper with no icons, apps or text. No people, no hands, no watermarks, no QR codes and no words other than the exact quoted strings above. Spell every Spanish word exactly as written, keeping accents (á, é, í, ó, ú), ñ, ¿ and ×; currency is always written S/ followed by a space.
```

- Títulos: Cable USB-C a Lightning a S/ 35 · 1 metro, sellado · Pagas al recibir en Lima

- Botón: Enviar mensaje
